If the available console width is no greater than `width + 1`, this calculation returns `0`. This will result in an error.\nThis bug might be related to the following code in `rich\columns.py` within rich.\n```\ndef __rich_console__:\n       ...\n        if self.width is not None:\n `ZeroDivisionError`.\n\n**Reproduction**\n```python\nfrom io import StringIO\n\nfrom rich.columns import Columns\nfrom rich.console import Console\n\nconsole = Console(\n    file=StringIO(),\n    width=16,\n    height=24,\n    force_terminal=True,\n    color_system=None,\n    highlight=False,\n)\nprint("size", console.width, "x", console.height)\nconsole.print(Columns([f"file_{i:02d}.txt" for i in range(8)], width=16))\n```\n\nObserved:\n\n```\nsize 16 x 24\nTraceback (most recent call last):\n  File "/home/test01.py", line 15, in <module>\n    console.print(Columns([f"file_{i:02d}.txt" `ZeroDivisionError`.\n\n**Reproduction**\n```python\nfrom io import StringIO\n\nfrom rich.columns import Columns\nfrom rich.console import Console\n\nconsole = Console(\n    file=StringIO(),\n    width=16,\n    height=24,\n    force_terminal=True,\n    color_system=None,\n    highlight=False,\n)\nprint("size", console.width, "x", console.height)\nconsole.print(Columns([f"file_{i:02d}.txt" for i in range(8)], width=16))\n```\n\nObserved:\n\n```\nsize 16 x 24\nTraceback (most recent call last):\n  File "/home/test01.py", line 15, in <module>\n    console.print(Columns([f"file_{i:02d}.txt" for i in range(8)], width=16))\n  File "/home/rich/rich/console.py", line 1731, in print\n    extend(render(renderable, render_options))\n  File "/home/rich/rich/console.py", line 1339, in render\n    for render_output in iter_render:\n  File "/home/rich/rich/columns.py", line 145, in __rich_console__\n    _renderables = [\n                   ^\n  File "/home/rich/rich/columns.py", line 115, in The binary comes first, so a malformed payload still runs the CLI and reports an error:
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5974434Z ```bash
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5974949Z safeoutputs add_comment '{"item_number":42,"body":"### Result\n\nIt\u0027s ready.\nQuoted text: \"hello\".\nLiteral path: C:\\temp\\file.txt"}'
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5975506Z ```
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5976245Z This preserves multiline content without auxiliary tools, pipelines, temporary files, or shell expansion. Adapt fields to the enabled tool's schema. Raw Markdown and `@filename` references are not JSON payloads.
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5976963Z 
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5995042Z Stdin is also supported: `safeoutputs <tool_name> .` reads a complete JSON object from stdin. CLI proxying grants the mounted executable, not auxiliary commands, pipelines, file writes, or shell expansions. Stdin support does not grant permission for redirections. Prefer inline JSON; do not introduce helper tools to construct or submit it.
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5996232Z 
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5998011Z If the sandbox rejects a command, do not repeat it or switch to an unapproved shell workaround. Use a native safe-output function only if explicitly exposed; otherwise call `safeoutputs report_incomplete` with inline JSON containing `reason` and optional `details` when reachable. If even that call is unavailable or denied, state the blocker in your final response; do not probe with real writes.
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5999319Z 
2026-10-25T02:33:45.5999790Z The generated command syntax above is schema-derived from each enabled tool's final `inputSchema` and is the source of truth for required/optional parameters.
2026-10-25T02:33:45.6000752Z Use `<server> --help` and `<server> <tool> --help` for the same schema-derived signatures and examples before calling any command.
2026-10-25T02:33:45.6001278Z </mcp-clis>
2026-10-25T02:33:45.6001479Z </system>
